- getSwingTradeSymbols default limit 200->50: reduces scan rows on swing symbol lookup
- New indexes: trades(strategy,side,timestamp), trades(alpaca_order_id), positions(closed_at)

**Monday, August 31, 2026 Control-255 strict read-only control + authorized reliability fix for Worker subrequest limit crash (HEALTHY code/deploy 2.6.0, DEGRADED external, deploy executed)**

Control-255 at ~20:00 UTC Aug 31 (22:00 +02). Market OPEN (closes 20:00 UTC). Strict GET-only on 6 endpoints (all 200: /health, /api/config, /api/dashboard, /api/positions, /api/runs, /api/trades). HEALTHY code/deploy (2.6.0), DEGRADED external. Subrequest limit crash found and fixed via authorized reliability fix + deploy.

DEFECT FOUND: Run 5544 @ 19:47:10 UTC (cron, market_open=0, dur=24442ms) hit "Fatal: Too many subrequests by single Worker invocation" — Cloudflare's hard subrequest limit killed the Worker mid-cycle during the EOD no-entry window (~13 min to close). The cycle was simultaneously (a) submitting closePosition orders for EOD flatten on all held positions, (b) fetching 200 bars for 25 held positions, and (c) fetching 200 bars for 20 new candidates — even though noNewEntries was active and all new BUY entries were already blocked by EOD_NO_ENTRY. The system self-recovered (runs 5545-5548 all ok/skipped, no stuck lease), but the crash is a real reliability defect.

FIX (reliability-only, caps/trading behavior preserved): (1) AlpacaClient now tracks outbound subrequests via trackedFetch() wrapper on all 9 fetch() calls, exposing getSubrequestCount(). (2) Universe scan skipped during EOD no-entry window (candidates cannot be entered anyway — saves 2 broker subrequests whose results are never used). (3) New candidate bar analysis skipped during EOD no-entry window (entries blocked by EOD_NO_ENTRY; held-position exit signals still analyzed from the earlier loop). (4) Subrequest budget guard: if subrequestsUsed >= 400, skip new candidate analysis regardless of EOD state. No caps changed, no trading behavior changed — entries were already blocked during EOD, exits still work. 224 tests/845 assertions pass, typecheck clean.

POST-DEPLOY VERIFICATION: separate read-only GET pass after deploy (see below).

**Monday, August 31, 2026 Control-241: swing cron DOW fix deployed and live-verified (HEALTHY code/deploy, DEGRADED external)**

Control-241 at ~08:12 UTC Aug 31 (10:12 +02). Strict GET-only control found a genuine schedule defect: the swing cron expression `0 22 * * 1-5` was firing Sunday-Thursday instead of Monday-Friday. Cloudflare cron DOW uses 1=Sunday through 7=Saturday (NOT Unix 0=Sunday), so 1-5 = Sun-Thu, not Mon-Fri. Evidence: swing_cron run 5120 fired Sunday Aug 30 22:00 UTC, run 3182 fired Sunday Aug 23; no swing run on Friday Aug 28 or Aug 21. Fix: changed to `0 22 * * 2-6` = Mon-Fri in wrangler.toml, src/index.ts dispatch match, and two test files. 224 tests / 845 assertions pass, typecheck clean. Deployed via direct CF API PUT (HTTP 200, success: true), live-verified: /health 200 v2.6.0, /api/config caps 5000/3700/2000 unchanged, all 4 schedules dispatching post-deploy (daytrading cron, reconcile_cron, crypto_cron confirmed). Code bd6205e. This is a reliability fix with no cap or trading-behavior change. Next swing_cron dispatch will be Monday Aug 31 22:00 UTC (today) — the first correct weekday dispatch.
